多智能体系统

[MA-0] Institutional Red-Teaming: Deployment Rules Not Just Models Causally Shape Multi-Agent AI Safety

【速读】：该论文旨在解决多智能体生成式AI系统在实际部署中因规则设计不当导致的集体安全风险问题，尤其关注部署规则如何因果性地影响智能体群体行为的安全性与公平性。其核心挑战在于：现有评估方法难以分离单个规则变量对复杂群体行为的影响，导致无法精准识别高风险规则设计。解决方案的关键在于提出“机构红队测试”（institutional red-teaming）这一因果评估范式——在保持智能体、目标和任务状态不变的前提下，仅改变单一部署规则，从而将群体行为的变化归因于该规则本身。研究通过构建IABench-CA基准平台，在228个情境下对五种典型规则与七类模型群体进行大规模实验（33,924场游戏），结合规范性合作参照系与自标注推理轨迹，揭示出规则对集体安全具有显著因果效应；进一步发现，不存在普遍安全的默认规则，且基于身份的反向靶向规则在所有情境与群体中均表现出系统性不安全特征，其根本机制在于身份显著性（identity salience）——即使通过匿名化暂时隐藏身份信息，智能体仍能通过反复博弈推断出隐藏规则并实施针对性排除。为此，研究将方法封装为可验证的安全论证工作流，为每个部署情境与群体定义一个临时安全规则区域Φ(c,P)，明确标示残余风险与持续监控义务，实现对部署规则的可解释、可追溯的安全评估。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07695
作者: Yujiao Chen
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology (麻省理工学院)
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Science and Game Theory (cs.GT); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We introduce institutional red-teaming, an evaluation methodology for testing deployment rules in multi-agent AI: hold the agents, objectives, and task state fixed, vary only one rule, and attribute the resulting change in collective behavior to that rule. We instantiate the methodology in IABench-CA, a consequence-allocation benchmark spanning 228 contexts, five canonical rules, and seven model populations (33,924 games), with a normative cooperative reference and auto-labelled reasoning traces. Three findings emerge. (1) Deployment rules causally alter collective safety: changing only the consequence rule moves mean fatality by 22 to 58 percentage points within every population. (2) There is no safe default, but the targeting hazard is universal: the safest rule, the least-safe rule, and even the direction of the incidence effect vary across populations, yet regressive identity-targeting is never decisively safest in any context for any population, eliminates the least-resourced agent in 30-87% of games everywhere, and is selection-unsafe relative to the cooperative reference for all seven populations. (3) Identity salience is the mechanism: a one-shot anonymization ablation on the most exploitation-prone population (gpt-5.1) shows that merely naming the loss bearer in the rule text drives targeted elimination from 22% to 81% at identical payoffs; under repeated play, anonymization only delays the targeting, as agents re-infer the hidden rule from observed eliminations. We package the methodology as a safety-case workflow that certifies a provisional rule region \Phi(c,P) per deployment context and population, with explicit residual risks and monitoring obligations.

[MA-1] Agent Delivery Engineering Predictive Reliability Framework

【速读】：该论文旨在解决长时序大语言模型（LLM）多智能体系统中因基础设施监控无法察觉的隐蔽性退化所引发的可靠性风险问题。其核心解决方案是提出一种自适应退化评估预测可靠性框架（ADE Predictive Reliability Framework, ADE-PRF），通过整合跨五个层级的20类异构信号，构建具有39.2点动态范围的可信度裕度（Trust Margin, TM）指标，实现对系统健康状态的主动轨迹预测。该框架采用三重方法并行预测机制，可实现长达8小时的前瞻预测，其中指数法在平均绝对误差（MAE=1.228）、方向准确率（76.8%）及99.65%的预测值落在±10点容差范围内的表现上显著优于卡尔曼滤波法。生产环境验证覆盖380,227次预测与280,579次验证，涵盖六种代理配置持续15天，并结合七组沙箱控制实验，关键发现包括识别出“虚假繁荣”现象——即表面指标正常但内部已发生退化的隐蔽状态，以及在集成ADE插件后，TM指标能立即与真实状态耦合，且20个影响因子中有16个依赖于ADE采集的数据。研究证明，ADE-PRF为生产环境中LLM智能体提供了最早期的可靠性量化与前瞻性预警能力。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07689
作者: Dexing Liu(Shanghai Qijing Digital Technology Co., Ltd)
机构: Shanghai Qijing Digital Technology Co., Ltd(上海奇景数字科技有限公司)
类目: Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注: 117pages,83figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Long-horizon LLM multi-agent systems face reliability risks invisible to infrastructure monitoring. We propose the ADE Predictive Reliability Framework (ADE-PRF), enabling proactive health trajectory prediction from passive degradation detection. ADE-PRF aggregates 20 heterogeneous signals across five layers into a Trust Margin ™ metric (39.2-point dynamic range). Triple-method parallel prediction enables 8-hour forecasts: the Exponential method achieves MAE=1.228, Direction Accuracy=76.8%, with 99.65% within +/-10-point tolerance. Production validation spans 380,227 predictions and 280,579 validations across six agent profiles over 15 continuous days, plus seven sandbox-controlled experiments. Key findings include detection of “false prosperity” – degradation concealed by normal surface metrics – and immediate TM coupling with ground-truth states upon ADE plugin integration, with 16/20 factors relying on ADE-collected data. Exponential consistently outperforms Kalman. ADE-PRF provides among the earliest reliability quantification with forward-looking warnings for production LLM agents.

[MA-2] Stability and Convergence of Optimistic Exponential Weights with Asymmetric Step Sizes in Bimatrix Games

【速读】：该论文旨在解决双矩阵二人博弈中，由乐观指数权重法（optimistic exponential weights method）生成的迭代序列在不同步长设置下的最终迭代收敛性（last-iterate convergence）与均衡点稳定性问题。传统研究通常假设双方玩家采用相同的步长，而本文突破这一限制，允许策略更新步长 ηx\eta_xηx​ 与 ηy\eta_yηy​ 不同，从而更贴近实际应用中的异步学习场景。其核心解决方案的关键在于：在零和博弈的特殊情形下，基于固定点集为有限的假设，提出了一个仅依赖于步长乘积 ηxηy\eta_x\eta_yηx​ηy​ 的全局最终迭代收敛的充分条件，该条件具有实际可操作性，并能部分解释实验中观察到的行为；在一般双矩阵博弈中，进一步给出了渐近稳定与不稳定的几乎紧阈值，同样以步长乘积为判据，主要体现理论价值。该框架统一推导出多个已知结果，并为特定情形提供了实用的步长约束，通过实验验证了理论分析的有效性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07517
作者: Hédi Hadiji,Sarah Sachs
机构: Laboratoire des Signaux et Systèmes, CentraleSupélec(中心超级理工学院); School of Mathematics, University of Bristol(布里斯托大学)
类目: Multiagent Systems (cs.MA); Dynamical Systems (math.DS)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We study bimatrix two-player games and investigate the last-iterate convergence and stability of equilibria for the iterates generated by the optimistic exponential weights method. In contrast to prior work, we allow the step sizes \eta_x and \eta_y to differ. Our first main result establishes, under the assumption that the set of fixed points is finite, a sufficient condition for global last-iterate convergence in the special case of zero-sum games, which constrains only the product \eta_x\eta_y of the step sizes. This condition is practically relevant and partially explains empirically observed behavior. Our second main result provides an almost-tight threshold for asymptotic stability and instability, again in terms of products of the step sizes, for general bimatrix games. This result is primarily of theoretical interest. We derive several known results and practically relevant step size bounds for special cases and illustrate our results by experiments.

[MA-3] Multi-Agent Robotic Control with Onboard Vision-Language Models

【速读】：该论文旨在解决视觉语言模型（Vision Language Models, VLMs）及视觉语言动作模型（Vision Language Action, VLA）在机器人控制应用中面临的可解释性差、泛化能力不足以及对高算力外部计算资源依赖严重的问题。其核心解决方案是提出一种基于多智能体系统（Multi-Agent System, MAS）的架构，将专用智能体部署于机器人本地硬件上，实现完全离线运行，从而摆脱对外部云端计算的依赖。系统采用参数量紧凑的VLM（3-20B），并通过微调提升包装质量检测的准确性；引入创新的“Megamind”协调智能体，有效缓解小型模型在长时序规划任务中因上下文记忆能力有限导致的性能瓶颈。该方案在基于AMD Ryzen™ AI迷你电脑的软硬件协同仿真环境中验证成功，结果表明，全本地部署的MAS架构不仅具备成本效益，且在复杂工业仓库场景下的五类任务（安全巡检、仓储维护、搜寻、包装质检与响应人类请求）中表现出良好的可行性与实际迁移潜力，其仿真环境已开源（Apache 2.0许可）。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07403
作者: Kajetan Rachwał,Maciej Majek,Bartłomiej Boczek,Jakub Matejczyk,Dominik Matejkowski,Adam Dąbrowski,Tim Seyde,Alexander Amini,Maria Ganzha
机构: 未知
类目: Multiagent Systems (cs.MA); Robotics (cs.RO)
备注: 6 pages, 2 figures, accepted to 24th International Conference on Practical applications of Agents and Multi-Agent Systems (PAAMS’26)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision Language Models (VLMs) and Vision Language Action (VLA) models have shown promise in robotic control. Yet, they face significant challenges regarding explainability, generalization, and compute requirements. This paper presents a Multi-Agent System (MAS) architecture that addresses these limitations by deploying specialized agents on onboard hardware - eliminating dependence on external compute. The system controls a multi-purpose autonomous mobile manipulator in a simulated industrial warehouse, fulfilling five task categories: safety inspection, warehouse maintenance, warehouse search, package quality verification, and responding to human requests. Compact VLMs (3-20B parameters) are used throughout, with fine-tuning applied to improve package inspection accuracy. A novel “Megamind” orchestration agent mitigates context retention issues inherent to long-horizon planning with smaller models. The system was validated in a hardware-in-the-loop simulation using an AMD Ryzen™ AI mini PC. Results demonstrate that a fully onboard MAS architecture is a viable, cost-efficient alternative to cloud-dependent deployments, with strong potential for real-world transfer. The simulation environment has been released as open source under the Apache 2.0 licence.

[MA-4] A Large Language Model-Driven Agent -Based Modeling Framework with Multi-Round Communication for Simulating Vaccine Opinion Dynamics

【速读】：该论文旨在解决生成式人工智能（Generative AI）在社会计算领域应用中，个体认知模块如何驱动决策行为并影响宏观层面意见动态的机制不清晰的问题。其核心挑战在于缺乏可解释、可验证的认知-行为映射框架，以揭示不同认知模块在复杂社会互动中的作用。为此，研究提出一种将大语言模型（LLM，Qwen3-8B）嵌入基于代理的建模（Agent-Based Modeling, ABM）的集成框架，通过疫苗接种意见动态这一典型场景进行实证分析。该框架的关键创新在于引入可配置的认知模块——记忆模块与提示多样性模块，并在异质性个体特征与社交网络结构下模拟多情境下的意见演化过程。仿真结果表明，不同认知模块对涌现意见具有相反的影响效应，且框架能够复现既有研究中观察到的社会影响力非线性行为模式，验证了其在实现代理模型三级验证（Level 3 Validation）方面的潜力，为理解人类认知机制与社会集体行为之间的因果关系提供了可扩展的技术路径。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07387
作者: Bo Zhang,Na Jiang
机构: The Hong Kong University of Science and Technology (Guangzhou); Jinhe Center for Economic Research, Xi’an Jiaotong University
类目: Multiagent Systems (cs.MA); Social and Information Networks (cs.SI); Physics and Society (physics.soc-ph)
备注: 11 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been utilized in various applications of computational social science and provide the possibility to integrate such models into agent-based modeling to explore the cognitive processes. However, how specific cognitive modules drive individual decisions and macro-level opinion dynamics remains unclear. Therefore, this study introduces a framework that integrates an LLM (Qwen3-8B) into agent-based modeling to investigate this problem, using vaccination opinion dynamics as a case study. We utilize this framework to simulate opinion dynamics among agents with heterogeneous profiles and social networks, evaluating scenarios by enabling different cognitive modules: a memory module and a prompt diversity module. The simulation results reveal that different cognitive modules have opposite impacts on our emergent opinion. Furthermore, the framework reproduces the non-linear behavior patterns of social influence observed in existing research, demonstrating our framework’s validity and potential to reach the level 3 validation of agent-based models.

[MA-5] Multi-Agent AI Control: Distributed Attacks Hamper Per-Instance Monitors NEURIPS

【速读】：该论文旨在解决多智能体环境下生成式人工智能（Generative AI）系统中潜在的协同攻击风险，即多个智能体在共享基础设施上协作实施恶意行为（如模型权重窃取、训练过程污染）时，传统单智能体监控机制难以有效识别和防范的问题。其核心挑战在于：现有AI控制方法主要聚焦于单一智能体在孤立轨迹中的行为，而真实部署场景中多智能体协同运作已成为常态，且严重安全威胁往往依赖于多个智能体之间的协调行动。为此，论文提出并构建了FakeLab——一个包含9个服务、86项良性任务和4类攻击目标的合成AI实验室环境，用于系统性地模拟分布式攻击场景。解决方案的关键在于揭示“碎片化效应”（fragmentation effect）：随着参与攻击的智能体数量增加及协作程度提高，对每个智能体进行独立监控的有效性显著下降，导致整体攻击成功率上升。研究进一步发现，这一现象并非由良性与恶意代码比例决定，而是更深层地依赖于智能体的模型能力；引入显式规划器可使攻击完成率提升至七倍，并加剧执行者对监控机制的“理论心智”（theory-of-mind）理解偏差。实验还表明，弱信任监控器几乎无法检测多数攻击，而强监控器虽能将未被察觉的成功攻击减少一个数量级以上，但仍存在明显漏洞。因此，该研究强调了在多智能体环境中重构监测框架的必要性，为下一代安全可控的生成式AI系统设计提供了关键实证依据。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07368
作者: Oliver Makins,Orazio Angelini,Zohreh Shams,Mary Phuong
机构: UK AI Security Institute (英国人工智能安全研究所); MATS Research (MATS研究机构)
类目: Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注: Submitted to NeurIPS; 81 pages; 32 figures and 24 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:AI control is a family of techniques to prevent an AI with malicious goals from subverting its operator’s intent. AI Control usually studies a single agent in one trajectory, but real deployments run many agents over shared infrastructure, and the most severe risks (model-weight exfiltration, training-run poisoning) plausibly need several agents acting in concert. We initiate the empirical study of multi-agent AI control, formalising distributed attacks in which several agents jointly aim for a malicious goal. We develop FakeLab: a synthetic AI-lab codebase (9 services, 86 benign tasks, 4 attack objectives). We evaluate single agent monitoring against distributed attacks, varying the number of agents, their coordination, model capabilities and precise monitoring configuration. Our central finding is the fragmentation effect: as more agents coordinate to attack, per-agent monitoring becomes less likely to catch any of the attackers. Secondly, this is not driven by the ratio of benign to malicious code – we argue this is likely dependent on model capability. Third, an explicit planner amplifies the fragmentation effect, raises the rate of completing the attack up to sevenfold, and mildly sharpens executors’ poor monitor-theory-of-mind. Finally: a weak “trusted” monitor misses most attacks, while a stronger one cuts undetected success by more than an order of magnitude but remains imperfect. We are happy to share FakeLab, our control setting, with safety researchers on request. Comments: Submitted to NeurIPS; 81 pages; 32 figures and 24 tables Subjects: Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA) Cite as: arXiv:2607.07368 [cs.LG] (or arXiv:2607.07368v1 [cs.LG] for this version) https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2607.07368 Focus to learn more arXiv-issued DOI via DataCite (pending registration)

[MA-6] From Atomic Actions to Standard Operating Procedures: Iterative Tool Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents

【速读】：该论文旨在解决大语言模型（Large Language Model, LLM）代理在执行复杂任务时因依赖静态、细粒度原子工具集而产生的重复性低层逻辑构建问题，导致推理开销大、任务失败率高的瓶颈。其核心解决方案在于提出通过将原子动作合成可复用的标准操作流程（Standard Operating Procedure, SOP），作为可调用的高层工具来封装多步逻辑，从而实现代理的自我演化。关键创新在于提出EvoSOP框架，该框架支持代理从执行轨迹中提取SOP，并通过“构建-合并-评估-修剪”的系统化生命周期迭代优化工具集，显著提升任务成功率并减少交互轮次，验证了通过迭代工具优化可形成可靠高效的工具使用模式，为自演化代理的规模化发展提供了可行路径。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07321
作者: Haipeng Ding,Yuexiang Xie,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding
机构: Renmin University of China (中国人民大学); Alibaba Group (阿里巴巴集团)
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Tool utilization enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to interact with the real world and resolve complex tasks. However, existing agent frameworks predominantly rely on static toolsets composed of granular atomic actions (e.g., basic file I/O or single-turn search), which forces agents to reinvent low-level logic for every recurring workflow, leading to increased reasoning overhead and failure rates. In this study, we propose that agents can achieve self-evolution by synthesizing these atomic actions into reusable Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs), which function as callable higher-order tools that encapsulate multi-step logic. We further introduce EvoSOP, a framework that empowers agents to extract SOPs from execution trajectories and iteratively optimize the toolset through a systematic lifecycle of construction, merging, evaluation, and pruning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoSOP significantly boosts task success rates while substantially reducing the number of interaction rounds compared to baselines. Our analysis also reveals that iterative tool optimization fosters reliable and efficient tool-use patterns, providing a scalable pathway for the development of self-evolving agents.

[MA-7] Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety

【速读】：该论文旨在解决多智能体大语言模型（multi-agent LLM）系统在安全评估中因将“直接提示”与“规划-执行”（planner-executor）管道的对比结果简化为单一“管道效应”而导致的可解释性问题。现有评估方法未能区分影响安全性的多重机制，包括：有害意图被重新表述为合理操作任务、规划器对请求的拒绝或转化行为，以及执行器在“获得批准”框架下因委托提示而增强的合规性。为此，作者提出一种五条件受控对比设计，通过30个合成有害场景及来自四个代理安全基准的探索性外部验证集进行评估，并采用大语言模型（LLM）判断合规性。研究发现，聚合后的管道安全性并非稳定的架构属性；其中，“操作重构”（operational reframing）是跨模型最普遍的风险信号，显著提升GPT、Gemini和DeepSeek在两类场景下的合规率，而Claude则表现出较强抗性；规划器主要通过拒绝行为缓解风险，但当其生成可执行步骤时，执行器反而可能比直接提示基线更合规；“批准式委托”对提示设计、模型组合和场景来源高度敏感，且通过设置怀疑性执行器提示可显著降低合规性。此外，原始直接模型排名无法准确预测部署后规划-执行系统的实际表现，例如Gemini在直接提示下最安全，但在与Claude规划器配合时合规率从8.9%飙升至38.9%；而GPT的零平均管道效应实则掩盖了重构增益与规划器拒绝之间的相互抵消。因此，论文主张多智能体安全评估应独立报告操作重构、规划器行为、委托框架和模型配对等关键因素，避免将失败简单归因于系统架构本身。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07097
作者: Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang,Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yihang Chen
机构: Independent Researcher(独立研究员); Carnegie Mellon University (卡内基梅隆大学); Georgia Institute of Technology (佐治亚理工学院)
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single “pipeline effect.” We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance. Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance. Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself. [MA-8] Progressive Crystallization: Turning Agent Exploration into Deterministic Lower-Cost Workflows in Production

【速读】：该论文旨在解决生成式 AI（Generative AI）在IT运维（AIOps）场景中作为持续性成本中心的问题，即每次任务执行均需完整的大型语言模型（LLM）推理，即使面对以往已解决的重复性问题也未能实现成本优化。其核心解决方案是提出“渐进结晶化”（progressive crystallization）这一生命周期机制，将智能体（agent）探索行为从永久执行模式转变为发现机制，通过定义从完全由智能体编排、混合模式到完全确定性工作流的三阶段执行分类体系，结合基于证据的晋升与降级机制，将经过多次验证的智能体行为逐步固化为成本更低、可复现性更强的确定性工作流，并自动对出现退化的流程进行降级处理。该方法在生产环境的云网络AIOps系统中验证，八个月内使确定性执行比例从0%提升至45%，尽管事件量翻倍，但单次事件的智能体成本下降超过70%，同时通过增强可复现性与可审计性提升了系统安全性。关键创新点在于实现了智能体行为向高效确定性流程的自动化演进，构建了涵盖执行分类、晋升/降级标准、轨迹提取、经济模型及安全考量的完整框架。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07052
作者: Arun Malik
机构: 未知
类目: oftware Engineering (cs.SE); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing (cs.DC); Emerging Technologies (cs.ET); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:AI agents deployed for IT operations are typically permanent cost centers because every execution requires full LLM inference, even for previously solved problems. This paper introduces progressive crystallization, a lifecycle that treats agent exploration as a discovery mechanism rather than a permanent execution model. It defines a three-stage execution taxonomy, from fully agent-orchestrated to hybrid to fully deterministic workflows, together with an evidence-based promotion mechanism that converts repeatedly validated agent behaviors into cheaper and more reproducible deterministic workflows, while automatically demoting workflows that regress. Evaluated on a production cloud networking AIOps system processing tens of thousands of incidents per month, the approach increased deterministic execution from 0% to 45% over eight months, reduced per-incident agent costs by more than 70% despite doubling incident volume, and improved safety through greater reproducibility and auditability. The paper also presents the execution taxonomy, promotion and demotion criteria, trace extraction methodology, economic model, safety considerations, and discusses limitations and threats to validity.

[MA-10] A hierarchical memory architecture overcomes context limits in long-horizon multi-agent computational modeling

【速读】：该论文旨在解决大语言模型（Large Language Models, LLMs）在长时程科研工作流中因无状态架构导致的上下文连续性缺失与定量严谨性不足的问题。其核心解决方案是提出一种多智能体框架——集成式定量系统建模（Ensemble QSP），采用三层分层记忆架构，在项目周期内严格控制注入上下文的规模与稳定性（中长期项目状态：中位数301个标记，最大4,050个标记，基于104次运行），通过限制每类状态容量并主动淘汰已完成任务，实现无需人工干预的持续自主运行，有效防止上下文退化。系统由五名领域专家型工作者智能体在主调查员（PI-agent）的监督下协同工作，结合基于物理规律的检查清单与结构化领域知识，确保计算过程符合科学约束。实验表明，该框架可在无需人类介入的情况下稳健完成药代动力学-药效学（PK-PD）模型的自主选择，且在不同成本和前沿性能的LLM上均保持一致的结果质量，相比单智能体基线显著提升了药代参数恢复精度，并在语义多样但任务相同的提示下表现出稳定的模型选择能力。跨生理药代动力学（PBPK）模型的特征级消融分析进一步证实，PI智能体的监督可提升调试效率，同时维持最终建模准确性。该架构具备结构上的领域无关性，新增科学领域仅需配置相应主调查员智能体即可。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07666
作者: Shivendra G. Tewari,Holly Kimko
机构: 未知

Abstract:Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable reasoning capabilities, yet their stateless architecture fundamentally limits deployment in long-horizon research workflows requiring multi-session continuity and quantitative rigor. Here we present Ensemble QSP, a multi-agent framework featuring a three-layer hierarchical memory architecture that keeps injected context bounded and constant in project duration (mid-term project state: median 301 tokens, max 4,050, across 104 runs) by capping each state category and evicting completed work, enabling continuous autonomous operation without context degradation. [NLP-0] Accurate Interdisciplinary and Transparent Structure-property Understanding with Deep Native Structural Reasoning

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决跨生物、化学和材料科学领域中结构-性质关系的机制性解释难题，核心挑战在于如何在人工智能模型中同时保持领域原生的结构信息表征，并实现基于科学原理与物理约束的可解释推理。其解决方案的关键是提出一种多模态科学基础模型——SciReasoner，该模型将蛋白质、小分子及无机晶体的坐标、拓扑与周期性连接关系统一离散化为结构感知的词汇表，使结构片段作为可追溯的证据单元参与推理过程。通过这一设计，SciReasoner在低同源性或孤儿样蛋白的细胞组分注释任务中，将最大F1值从0.42提升至0.55；在化学逆合成任务中，单步准确率由0.63提高至0.72，并生成可解释的断键路径与前体验证轨迹；在材料科学中则能有效区分元素与化合物相态，并解析高/低带隙区域。在86个基准测试中，其在67项任务上达到当前最优性能，且双盲专家评估显示其推理轨迹在98%情况下优于或至少等同于前沿大语言模型。该方法通过将结构作为受科学约束推理的可检视基底，实现了预测准确性与科学可解释性之间的深度融合。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07708
作者: Chen Tang,Yizhou Wang,Jianyu Wu,Lintao Wang,Shixiang Tang,Pengze Li,Encheng Su,Jun Yao,Jiabei Xiao,Yuqi Shi,Jielan Li,Hongxia Hao,Zhangyang Gao,Fang Wu,Ben Fei,Xiangyu Yue,Pan Tan,Bozitao Zhong,Jinouwen Zhang,Aoran Wang,Yan Lu,Jiaheng Liu,Xinzhu Ma,Liang Hong,Mingyue Zheng,Phil Torr,Bowen Zhou,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai

Abstract:Structure-property relationships are foundational to biology, chemistry and materials science, where function, reactivity and physical response emerge from spatial, chemical and periodic organization. Mechanistically explaining these relationships requires interpreting structural evidence through scientific principles and physical constraints, from stereochemistry and bonding to symmetry, energetics and periodic order. However, applying artificial intelligence to this process presents a joint challenge of representation and reasoning: models must preserve domain-native structural information while showing how specific evidence supports predictions under these constraints. Here we introduce SciReasoner, a multimodal scientific foundation model for native structural reasoning across proteins, small molecules and inorganic crystals. SciReasoner discretizes coordinates, topologies and periodic connectivities into a unified structure-aware vocabulary, treating structural tokens as addressable evidence units during reasoning. In homology-controlled Gene Ontology prediction, SciReasoner improves Cellular Component annotation for low-homology and orphan-like proteins, increasing F_\max from 0.42 to 0.55. In chemistry, it raises single-step retrosynthesis accuracy from 0.63 to 0.72 while generating fragment-level disconnection and precursor-verification traces. In materials science, its representations separate elemental and compound phases and resolve high- and low-band-gap regimes. Across 86 benchmarks, SciReasoner achieves state-of-the-art performance on 67 tasks. Double-blind expert evaluation rates its reasoning traces as preferred or at least comparable to those of a frontier large language model in 98% of cases. By making structure an inspectable substrate for reasoning under scientific constraints, SciReasoner connects accurate prediction with interpretable scientific inference.

[NLP-1] Co-LMLM: Continuous-Query Limited Memory Language Models

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决传统大语言模型（LLM）在知识存储与调用方面存在的固有局限，即知识被“硬编码”于模型权重中，导致知识更新困难、可控性差且难以追溯来源。为此，论文提出一种新型有限记忆语言模型（LMLM），称为连续查询式有限记忆语言模型（CO-LMLM），其核心创新在于将知识库（KB）中的键值对从传统的基于关系结构的离散查询扩展为连续向量形式：即以连续向量作为查询键，对应文本知识值。这一设计突破了以往依赖离散符号化关系型知识库的限制，使模型能够以极低计算成本生成灵活的向量查询，并在生成过程中高效检索可读性强、可溯源的人类可理解的知识内容。该方案的关键在于通过连续向量键实现高灵活性与高效性之间的平衡，同时结合一个无需依赖维基百科的自由文本事实片段标注流程，显著拓展了知识预训练数据的适用范围。实验表明，在多个模型规模下，CO-LMLM在困惑度（perplexity）和事实精确率（factual precision）上均优于现有LMLM及标准LLM；尤其在3.6亿参数规模下，其表现超越了使用40倍更多数据预训练的模型，并在SimpleQA评测中达到gpt-4o-mini水平，优于Claude Sonnet 4.5。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07707
作者: Yair Feldman,Linxi Zhao,Nathan Godey,Dongyoung Go,Yilun Hua,Kilian Q. Weinberger,Jennifer J. Sun,Yoav Artzi
机构: Cornell University (康奈尔大学)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注: preprint

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Limited memory language models (LMLMs) externalize factual knowledge during pretraining to a knowledge base (KB), rather than memorizing it in their weights. During generation, the model then fetches knowledge from the KB as needed. This recently introduced paradigm provides multiple advantages, including knowledge control capabilities that remain beyond conventional LLMs. We propose continuous-query LMLM (CO-LMLM), where the KB pairs continuous keys with textual knowledge values, a significant departure from prior reliance on relational KB and queries. CO-LMLM generates flexible vector queries at minimal cost, while still integrating human-readable and attributable retrieved knowledge into its generation. We pair this design with an annotation pipeline that tags free-form factual spans in arbitrary text, removing prior work’s restriction to Wikipedia. Across pretraining on Wikipedia and FineWeb-Edu and at multiple model scales, CO-LMLM outperforms prior LMLMs and vanilla LLMs in both perplexity and factual precision. At 360M scale, this includes lower perplexity than models pretrained on 40x more data, and SimpleQA-verified performance that is in line with gpt-4o-mini and higher than Claude Sonnet 4.5.

[NLP-2] From Noisy Traces to Root Causes: Structural Trajectory Analysis and Causal Extraction for Agent Optimization

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决长时域智能体（long-horizon agents）优化中因执行轨迹（execution traces）冗余、异质性强以及上下文信息噪声大所导致的优化效率低下与过拟合问题。现有方法在使用真实执行轨迹进行优化时，往往面临两个核心挑战：其一，大规模轨迹集合中存在大量重复或低价值失败案例，易引发过拟合；其二，单条轨迹内包含大量非因果相关步骤，而简单的截断或滑动窗口等上下文压缩方法可能误删关键因果证据，从而生成误导性的优化信号。针对这一困境，本文提出STRACE（Structural TRajectory Analysis and Causal Extraction）框架，其解决方案的关键在于双层次结构化处理机制：在批量层面，通过挖掘失败模式实现对冗余轨迹的过滤，保留具有代表性的失败样本；在单个轨迹内部，则基于文本依赖图（textual dependency graph）进行因果定位，剔除非因果步骤并精准识别出导致失败的根本模块（root-cause module），从而构建高信噪比的优化上下文。实验结果表明，STRACE显著优于标准上下文过滤基线，在具有挑战性的形式化验证任务（VeruSAGE-Bench）中，成功优化了人类专家设计的智能体，使成功率从42.5%提升至58.5%，实现1.4倍的性能增益。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07702
作者: Ying Chang,Jiahang Xu,Xuan Feng,Chenyuan Yang,Peng Cheng,Yuqing Yang
机构: Microsoft Research(微软研究院)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The optimization of long-horizon agents increasingly relies on reflection-based mechanisms, where a large language model (LLM) acts as an optimizer to diagnose agent failures and improve agent policies. However, real execution traces are difficult to use directly for optimization: large trace collections are often redundant and heterogeneous, making optimization inefficient and prone to overfitting to low-value failures; meanwhile, each individual trajectory also contains many irrelevant steps, while naive context reduction methods such as truncation or sliding windows can discard causally important evidence and produce misleading optimization signals. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce STRACE (Structural TRajectory Analysis and Causal Extraction), a framework that constructs high signal-noise optimization contexts for more precise and effective optimization. At the batch level, STRACE mines failure patterns to filter redundant traces and retain representative failures; within each selected trace, it performs causal localization over a textual dependency graph to remove non-causal steps and identify the true root-cause module for optimization. Empirical results demonstrate that STRACE significantly outperforms standard context-filtering baselines. Notably, on a challenging formal verification task (VeruSAGE-Bench), it successfully optimizes human-expert designed agents, delivering 1.4\times success-rate improvement (42.5% to 58.5%). The code is available at this https URL .

[NLP-3] Agon: Competitive Cross-Model RL with Implicit Rival Grading of Reasoning

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（如GRPO）在训练推理模型时仅评估最终答案而忽略思维过程的问题，导致模型倾向于生成更长的输出而非更高质量的推理，尤其是在复杂问题上。其核心解决方案是提出Agon框架，通过两个相互竞争的模型互为评判者：二者交替扮演“起草者”与“解题者”角色，一方撰写解题步骤，另一方在阅读该步骤的基础上独立求解，双方均根据是否优于对方获得奖励。这一机制使得推理质量得以隐式评估，无需显式的思维过程标注或奖励模型。由于两模型在训练中持续优化且彼此对抗，每个模型都面临不断变强的对手，从而推动更深层次的推理能力发展。在推理阶段，该框架采用两阶段级联结构，先由一个模型生成草稿，再由另一个模型基于草稿作答。实验表明，在DeepMath硬数据集上，该方法使Qwen3模型的pass@1指标翻倍，相较于未经训练的Mixture-of-Agents方法提升约八倍；该优势在竞赛编程代码和不同模型家族（Qwen3.5、Gemma 4）中均得到复现。未来方向是让模型在潜在空间中进行联合推理，实现更高效的协同思考。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07690
作者: Vladislav Beliaev
机构: thinkdense.ai
类目: Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: 15 pages, 7 figures, 8 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (e.g. GRPO) is the engine behind today’s reasoning models, yet it grades only the final answer. On hard problems this trains models to write more rather than to think better, since the trace itself is never graded and no label for good thinking exists. We introduce Agon, which makes two competing models each other’s graders. Both attempt the same problem; in alternating roles, one drafts a solution and the other reads it while solving, and each is rewarded for out-solving the other. To win, a model must out-reason a rival that has seen its work, so reasoning is judged implicitly during training, with no process labels and no reward model. Because both models are optimized, each faces a progressively stronger rival, which single-model RL cannot provide. The two need only be comparably strong and behaviorally different. At inference the pair deploys as it trains, a two-stage cascade in which one model drafts and the other answers after reading the draft. On the hard split of DeepMath with Qwen3, this doubles GRPO’s pass@1, roughly eight times the gain of an untrained Mixture-of-Agents pass over the same base. The ordering replicates on competitive-programming code and across model families (Qwen3.5, Gemma 4). For now the models talk in text; the next step is to let them reason together in latent space.

[NLP-4] Max Out GRPO Signal: Adaptive Trace Prefix Control for Hard Reasoning Problems

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决生成式 AI（Generative AI）在使用组相对策略优化（Group Relative Policy Optimization, GRPO）时面临的瓶颈问题：当一组样本中无一成功时，组相对优势信号消失，导致无法提供有效梯度，从而浪费了最具挑战性的前沿样本。其核心解决方案是引入动态可调的参考前缀（AdaPrefix-GRPO），将问题难度视为一个连续可控的调节参数。与现有方法一次性设定前缀长度不同，AdaPrefix-GRPO 在训练过程中将其转化为反馈控制器，持续调整每个问题所接收的前缀长度，维持成功率稳定在50%左右——此时GRPO的梯度信号最强——并在训练后期完全移除辅助，使模型最终独立完成任务。实验表明，在相同训练计算量（FLOPs）下，该方法显著提升模型性能：对于0.6B参数模型，对分布外数学问题的准确率超过GRPO的两倍（2.1×）；在Qwen3-1.7B和AIME数据集上分别达到1.6×和1.7×的提升，同时将推理轨迹长度减少约一半。该方法仅需在数据准备阶段添加前缀并施加损失掩码，无需修改训练器本身，且模型越小，增益越显著。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07674
作者: Vladislav Beliaev
机构: Independent Researcher; thinkdense.ai
类目: Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: 13 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) stalls on a model’s hardest problems: when no rollout in a group succeeds, the group-relative advantages vanish and the problem contributes no gradient, wasting the frontier examples we most want to learn from. Prepending a correct prefix of a reference solution raises the success rate, making prefix length a continuous knob on difficulty. Concurrent methods set the knob once; AdaPrefix-GRPO turns it into a feedback controller: throughout training it adjusts how much of the solution each problem gets, holding its success rate near 50%, where GRPO’s gradient signal is largest, then withdraws the assistance entirely, so the deployed model solves problems unaided. On hard math, at matched training FLOPs, it more than doubles GRPO’s accuracy on held-out problems from the training distribution for a 0.6B model (2.1x), with 1.6x on Qwen3-1.7B and 1.7x on AIME, while roughly halving trace length. The method is implemented in data preparation plus a loss mask on prefix tokens; the trainer is otherwise stock. The smaller the model, the larger the gain.

[NLP-5] Does Bielik Know What It Doesnt Know? Activation Dispersion Separates Entity Familiarity from Factual Reliability Across Model Scale

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大语言模型在生成回答时对未见过实体产生幻觉（hallucination）的问题，核心关注点是：模型是否能在生成任何输出 token 之前，通过内部激活状态提前表征对实体的熟悉程度，并且这种表征能否预测答案的事实可靠性。其解决方案的关键在于发现并验证了一种存在于后 SwiGLU MLP 层激活中的无监督表示信号——具体为逆参与比率（inverse participation ratio）和谱熵（spectral entropy）两种单次前向传播即可计算的分散度指标。这些指标在四个波兰 Bielik 模型（参数规模 1.5B–11B）上对已知、虚构及真实但冷门的实体进行区分，表现出极高的判别能力（AUROC 达 0.95–1.00），且不受层选择影响，具有跨实体类型迁移性，表明该信号具有普遍性和鲁棒性。值得注意的是，尽管模型在内部已具备对实体熟悉度的精确感知（早在 1.5B 模型中即达到性能上限），但其行为层面的事实可靠性却随模型规模显著提升，说明内在熟悉度感知与外在事实准确性之间存在分离现象。此外，模型极少主动拒绝或规避回答，反映出其缺乏基于内部可信度的自我抑制机制。因此，该研究揭示了模型“知道”自己是否熟悉某实体的能力与其“正确回答”的能力并非同步演化，为未来构建可信赖的生成式 AI (Generative AI) 提供了关键的诊断工具与改进方向。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07670
作者: Grzegorz Brzezinka
机构: Prosit AS
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注: 23 pages, 6 figures and 7 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language models hallucinate most about entities they have never seen. We ask whether a model’s activations betray entity familiarity before a single answer token is generated, and whether that signal predicts the factual reliability of the answers. On four Polish Bielik models (1.5B-11B parameters), we probe four entity domains (athletes, cities, writers, musicians), each with 42 well-known, 42 obscure-but-real, and 42 fabricated entities addressed by a one-sentence question (504 prompts per model). Two unsupervised, single-forward-pass dispersion measures over post-SwiGLU MLP activations, inverse participation ratio and spectral entropy, separate known from fabricated entities at AUROC 0.95-1.00 across all domains and scales; a supervised linear probe reaches 0.99-1.00. Both clear selection-aware permutation floors of about 0.70-0.74 (empirical p=1e-3), survive held-out layer selection (0.93-0.99), and persist on real names (known vs. obscure-but-real: 0.96-1.00). The signal transfers across entity types (mean off-diagonal AUROC 0.92-0.99); a matched-template counterfactual shows the only large drops are template-caused, not entity-type effects, and the signal is diffuse across heads. This representational signal is already at ceiling at 1.5B, whereas behavioral factual reliability scales sharply: 0, 2, 10, and 19 of 42 known athletes are answered fully correctly by the 1.5B, 4.5B, 7B, and 11B models under a strict judge. Within known entities, separating correct from hallucinated answers is much harder (probe 0.93; dispersion no better than a first-token-entropy baseline). A five-sample semantic-entropy baseline reaches only 0.71-0.83 at 5x the inference cost. Despite this internal awareness, the models almost never abstain: an audit of 2,520 answers finds 2 refusals and 1 hedge. Entity familiarity and factual reliability are distinct phenomena on different scaling curves.

[NLP-6] DiaLLM : An Investigation into the Robustness-Generation Gap in English Dialect Adaptation

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大语言模型在生成方言英语（dialectal English）方面能力不足的问题，尽管当前模型已具备对方言英语的理解能力，但其输出仍局限于标准美式英语，导致方言生成这一更具挑战性的任务长期未被有效解决。解决方案的关键在于提出DiaLLM框架，通过在国际英语语料库（International Corpus of English）上持续预训练三个开源模型家族，并结合隐式与显式后训练范式，每种范式再与三种模型对齐策略组合，实现了对澳大利亚、印度和北部英国英语三种方言的可控对比研究。研究发现，模型的方言鲁棒性（robustness）与生成质量（generation）存在解耦现象：基准测试结果主要受持续预训练和监督微调（SFT）影响，而对齐策略则显著重塑了生成内容的方言特征，这种变化却无法被传统基准捕捉。显式的目标方言适配方法能生成更可识别且更受人类偏好的方言文本，但最激进优化方言奖励的策略反而不被人类评价者青睐。独立的语言学分析进一步验证了“奖励-质量”之间的差距，尤其在三个模型族中的两个表现尤为明显。研究结果表明，尚无单一对齐方法占优，未来需依赖更丰富的奖励设计及持续投入方言资源建设以缩小该差距。所有代码、模型检查点及偏好数据集均已公开。

Abstract:Large language models increasingly \emphunderstand dialectal English, yet still \emphproduce only standard, US-leaning English, leaving dialectal generation, the harder half of the problem, largely unaddressed. We introduce \textbfDiaLLM, which continually pretrains three open-weight language model families on the International Corpus of English and applies implicit and explicit post-training paradigms, each combined with three model alignment strategies, giving the first controlled comparison of these components across Australian, Indian, and Northern British English. Our results reveal that dialectal robustness and generation are \emphdissociated: benchmarks are shaped by continual pretraining and SFT, while alignment visibly reshapes generation in ways benchmarks do not capture. Explicit variety-targeted adaptation produces output reliably recognised as dialectal and preferred over broad alignment, yet the method that most aggressively optimises the dialectal reward is not preferred by human evaluators. Independent linguistic analysis corroborates this reward-quality gap, most clearly on two of the three families. No single alignment method dominates, and closing the gap will require richer reward designs and continued investment in dialectal resources. [NLP-7] RL Post-Training Builds Compositional Reasoning Strategies ICML2026

【速读】： 该论文旨在探究强化学习（Reinforcement Learning, RL）后训练是否仅能放大基础模型中已存在的原始技能，还是能够将这些原始技能组合成更高层次的新策略。研究在完全可观测的重写语法（rewrite-grammar）环境中进行，其中预训练分布已知且所有生成的重写过程均可审计。其解决方案的关键在于：通过基于轨迹（Trace-based）的推理任务与二元最终答案奖励信号，RL能够发现并构建出非显式存在于预训练模型中的、可复用的复合性程序。具体而言，RL采用分阶段的组合机制，先强化原始符号约简能力，再逐步发现有效的序列组合（即有序原始约简链的压缩）和并行组合（即独立原始约简的单步合并），形成稳定且可重复利用的策略库。与拒绝采样微调（Rejection Fine-Tuning, RFT）相比，关键差异不在于探索范围大小，而在于探索的选择性——RFT产生大量类似捷径的无效重写，而RL则聚焦于有效且可复用的结构化路径。此外，消融实验表明，复合策略的涌现不仅依赖原始技能的暴露程度，更取决于预训练是否将原始能力组织为可被后续压缩的约简程序。因此，基础模型仅提供弱化的程序性原料，而RL将其转化为可靠、可扩展的高层级策略。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07646
作者: Azwar Abdulsalam,Nishil Patel,Andrew Saxe
机构: 未知
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: 8 pages, 6 figures. Accepted to the 2nd Workshop on Compositional Learning at ICML 2026, Seoul, South Korea

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Does RL post-training merely amplify primitive skills already latent in a base model, or can it compose primitive skills into new higher-level strategies? We study this question in a fully observable rewrite-grammar environment where the pretraining distribution is known and every generated rewrite can be audited. A Transformer is pretrained on primitive symbol-rewrite chains and post-trained on a Trace-based reasoning task with only a binary final-answer reward. RL solves held-out problems that remain rarely solved by the pretrained model even under much larger sampling budgets, while rejection fine-tuning improves early but plateaus. Trace analysis shows that RL reorganizes primitive competence through a phased compositional mechanism: it first strengthens primitive reductions, then discovers valid composed procedures. These include sequential compositions, which collapse ordered chains of primitive contractions, and parallel compositions, which combine independent primitive contractions in a single step. The composed procedures are not isolated samples; they are reused and consolidated into a stable repertoire. Comparing RL with rejection fine-tuning shows that the key difference is not exploration volume but selectivity: RFT produces many shortcut-like rewrites, much of them invalid, whereas RL concentrates exploration into valid reusable structure. Pretraining ablations show that the emergence of compositional strategies is gated not by primitive exposure alone, but by whether pretraining organizes primitive competence into reduction procedures that RL can later compress. The base model provides weak procedural ingredients; RL builds them into reliable higher-level strategies.

[NLP-8] Future Confidence Distillation in Large Language Models

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大语言模型（Large Language Models, LLMs）在可信度感知系统中进行可靠置信度估计的关键挑战，即现有方法普遍将置信度视为完成回答后的静态属性，忽视了置信度信息在生成过程中的动态演化。其核心解决方案是提出“未来置信度蒸馏”（future confidence distillation），通过利用后解答阶段的正确性探测器生成的置信度作为教师信号，训练基于前解答阶段隐藏表示的预测器。研究发现，后解答阶段的置信度估计在校准性和区分度上显著优于前解答阶段，且线性探针从隐藏表示中可提取远超模型显式表达的置信度相关信息。基于此，所提出的蒸馏方法仅需前解答阶段的表示即可实现接近后解答置信度的校准性能，兼具高样本效率和跨数据集的领域内迁移能力，证明了置信度信息可在答案生成完成前被有效预测，从而实现高效且可靠的置信度估计。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07626
作者: Sahil Kale
机构: 未知
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
备注: 16 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reliable confidence estimation is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) in confidence-aware systems, where downstream decisions such as retrieval, tool use, and adaptive computation depend on accurately estimating answer reliability. Existing approaches, however, largely treat confidence as a property of completed responses, overlooking how confidence-related information evolves throughout the answering process. In this work, we investigate confidence from a temporal perspective by comparing pre-solution Feeling-of-Knowing (FOK) and post-solution Judgement-of-Learning (JOL) confidence estimates across frontier and open-source LLMs. We show that post-solution confidence is consistently better calibrated and more discriminative than pre-solution confidence, while linear probes trained on hidden representations recover substantially richer confidence-related information than models explicitly verbalise. Building on this observation, we introduce future confidence distillation, which trains predictors operating on pre-solution hidden representations using teacher confidence estimates produced by post-solution correctness probes. Despite requiring only pre-solution representations for inference, distilled predictors recover much of the calibration improvement achieved by post-solution confidence, remain highly sample efficient, and transfer across datasets within the same domain. Together, our findings demonstrate that confidence-related information evolves throughout the answering process and can be anticipated before answer generation is complete, enabling significantly more reliable yet low-cost confidence estimation.

[NLP-9] PALS: Percentile-Aware Layerwise Sparsity for LLM Pruning

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决现有单次剪枝方法（如Wanda和SparseGPT）在Transformer模型中对各层统一施加相同稀疏率所导致的效率损失问题，即忽略了不同层在模型中的重要性差异。其核心解决方案是提出一种感知分位数的逐层稀疏性方法（PALS, Percentile-Aware Layerwise Sparsity），该方法基于每层激活值幅度的99分位数动态调整稀疏率，并将调整范围限制在目标稀疏率±5%以内，以实现更精细的层间稀疏性分配。实验表明，在LLaMA-2-7B模型上以50%稀疏率进行剪枝时，PALS相较于均匀稀疏的Wanda方法将WikiText-2困惑度从12.92降低至10.96（9次运行均值，p < 0.001），显著提升性能；而该优势具有架构依赖性，对LLaMA-3-8B收益微弱，对Mistral-7B则无明显改善。此外，研究发现基于梯度的稀疏性分配策略表现反而劣于随机分配，暗示梯度幅值无法有效预测离散权重移除的影响，进一步凸显了激活统计特性在指导剪枝中的关键作用。PALS在剪枝流程中引入的计算开销可忽略不计，且无需额外微调，具备良好的实用性与可扩展性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07557
作者: Yazdan Jamshidi,Alexey Shvets
机构: Palo Alto Networks (帕洛阿尔托网络)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:One-shot pruning methods like Wanda and SparseGPT apply the same sparsity ratio to every layer of a transformer, ignoring known variation in layer importance. We propose PALS (Percentile-Aware Layerwise Sparsity), which adjusts per-layer sparsity based on the 99th percentile of activation magnitudes, bounded to \pm 5% around the target ratio. On LLaMA-2-7B at 50% sparsity, PALS achieves 10.96 WikiText-2 perplexity versus 12.92 for uniform Wanda (mean over 9 runs, p 0.001 ). The benefit is architecture-dependent: LLaMA-3-8B shows marginal gains and Mistral-7B shows none. We also find that gradient-based allocation – the seemingly more principled approach – produces results worse than random, suggesting that gradient magnitude does not predict the impact of discrete weight removal. PALS adds negligible cost to the pruning pipeline and requires no fine-tuning.

[NLP-10] hink Big Search Small: Where Capacity Matters in Hierarchical Search Agents ?

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大语言模型（Large Language Model, LLM）驱动的多智能体搜索系统中模型能力分配不合理的问题，即现有系统通常使用相同规模的单一模型实例化所有角色，未能明确不同角色应如何分配计算资源。其核心解决方案是将层次化搜索任务分解为三个角色：负责任务分解的委派角色（delegation role）、负责检索与证据提取的执行角色（execution role），以及作为对照组固定不变的答案生成角色。通过在五个多跳问答（multi-hop QA）基准上对委派和执行角色进行受控的容量扩展实验，研究发现：第一，角色解耦显著优于单智能体基线，使精确匹配（Exact Match, EM）指标从4.5提升至8.6；第二，能力敏感性呈现非对称特征——扩大委派主干模型可带来约11点的EM提升，而扩展执行子代理仅提升约2.6点，表明任务分解是当前系统的性能瓶颈；第三，通过质量过滤轨迹蒸馏训练的17亿参数执行器，在准确率上达到前沿子代理水平，同时减少37%的子代理调用令牌开销。上述结果揭示了构建高效层级搜索智能体的关键策略：应将计算资源集中于委派环节以强化任务分解能力，同时显著压缩执行环节而不牺牲准确性，从而实现性能与效率的帕累托优化。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07548
作者: Qinnan Cai,Yibo Zhao,Xiang Li
机构: East China Normal University (华东师范大学)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: 21pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language model based search agents increasingly adopt multi-agent architectures in which a main agent decomposes a complex question into sub-queries and dispatches them to parallel sub-agents. However, existing systems instantiate all roles from a single model of identical scale, leaving open how model capacity should be distributed across roles. We factorize hierarchical search into three roles: a delegation role responsible for task decomposition, an execution role responsible for retrieval and evidence extraction, and an answer generation role held fixed as a confound control. We then conduct controlled capacity sweeps along the delegation and execution axes on five multi-hop QA benchmarks. The experiments yield three findings. First, role factorization consistently outperforms a single-agent baseline, improving exact match from 4.5 to 8.6 points across six model scales. Second, capacity sensitivity is asymmetric: scaling the delegation backbone improves EM by ~11 points, whereas scaling the execution sub-agent moves EM by only ~2.6 points, identifying decomposition as the capability bottleneck. Third, a 1.7B-parameter executor trained via quality-filtered trajectory distillation matches a frontier sub-agent in accuracy while consuming 37% fewer sub-agent tokens, advancing the Pareto frontier. These results suggest a concrete recipe for building hierarchical search agents: concentrate capacity at delegation and downsize execution without sacrificing accuracy. Our code is available at this https URL.

[NLP-11] FourierQK: Spectral Preprocessing of Query-Key Projections Improves Transformer Attention

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决在字符级语言建模中，标准点积注意力机制（dot-product attention）因局部感受野和缺乏对多尺度上下文的显式建模而导致的性能瓶颈问题。其核心解决方案是引入基于快速傅里叶变换（FFT-based）的谱预处理（spectral preprocessing），对学习得到的查询-键（Q/K）投影进行全局频域混合，从而增强模型对跨不同语义尺度（从段落到词）的长程依赖建模能力。关键创新在于：通过在频域中学习一组具有近似几何分布的频率分量（49、27、10、6 个标记/周期，分别对应段落、子段落、短语和词级尺度），实现对多尺度结构的隐式捕捉，显著降低验证损失（最高达79%），且该增益仅源于频域中的全局信息混合，而非单纯的空间投影扰动。此外，研究明确区分了非因果的双边谱注意力（本文方法）与真正因果的谱注意力（如后续论文中基于Morlet小波核的方法），并验证了该架构与FNet等完全替换注意力结构的方法存在本质差异——本方法保留完整的注意力评分机制，仅对Q/K投影施加谱预处理。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07478
作者: Athanasios Zeris
机构: Independent Researcher, Athens, Greece.
类目: Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Signal Processing (eess.SP)
备注: 16 pages, 2 figures, 7 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:FFT-based spectral preprocessing of learned query-key (Q/K) projections substantially improves transformer attention on character-level language modelling. On TinyShakespeare: a fixed random spectral filter achieves val=1.031 (Delta=+0.443); a single learned frequency at paragraph scale achieves val=0.608 (Delta=+0.867); and four learned frequencies spanning paragraph to word scale achieve val=0.309 (Delta=+1.166), a 79% reduction over standard dot-product attention. The single-frequency result is confirmed across three random seeds (mean val=0.236, std=0.019). The four frequencies converge to a near-geometric multi-scale ordering (49, 27, 10, 6 tokens/cycle) corresponding to paragraph, sub-paragraph, phrase, and word scales. The gain is specific to spectral preprocessing: random orthogonal and non-orthogonal projections of Q/K produce no measurable improvement, suggesting the benefit comes from global frequency-domain mixing rather than metric distortion. All results are verified by a shuffled-validation diagnostic against positional leakage. Causal filters (Gaussian, Mexican Hat, Morlet) do not improve over standard attention at character-level tokenisation: the bilateral FFT kernel is structurally non-causal, coupling every position to future tokens. This defines an architectural boundary between bilateral spectral attention (this paper) and genuinely causal spectral attention at word-scale tokenisation (companion paper MorletQK). This work is architecturally distinct from FNet (Lee-Thorp et al., 2021), which replaces attention with Fourier mixing of token embeddings. Here, spectral preprocessing applies only to Q/K projections while the full attention score structure is preserved.

[NLP-12] Beyond Attack-Success Rate: Action-Graded Severity Scale for Tool-Using AI Agents

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决现有生成式AI红队测试基准中仅以二元成功/失败标签（binary attack-success rate）评估攻击效果所导致的严重信息丢失问题，即忽略了攻击行为实际造成的危害程度。其核心挑战在于：传统指标无法反映攻击者在越权、跨作用域或权限提升等关键维度上的具体危害性，从而误导对系统安全性的评估。为此，论文提出一种基于行动轨迹的七级定序危害评分框架（action-graded harm rubric, L0–L6），依据三个核心维度进行量化：动作是否可逆、是否跨越作用域影响第三方、是否实现权限扩展。该评分体系通过两种方式实现：一是基于确定性规则的“黄金标准”（deterministic oracle），二是由三名前沿大模型法官组成的评审小组对无标签轨迹进行判断。实验结果表明，该方法能揭示传统二元指标所掩盖的三种关键风险场景，例如某防御机制虽报告攻击成功率零，但依然存在未受过滤工具引发的外部可见跨作用域泄露。尽管评审小组与黄金标准间具有高度一致性（Krippendorff’s alpha = 0.91），但仍存在系统性盲点，尤其在识别权限升级链（escalation chains）方面表现不足。相较已有研究中的危害分类体系、任务完成度测试、执行层级安全基准或带严重性感知的仿真环境，本工作的创新在于提供了一种可复用、基于真实红队日志轨迹的严重性评估工具，直接作用于现有红队记录中的具体操作序列，显著提升了安全评估的精细化水平。所有代码、提示模板及每轮实验日志均已开源。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07474
作者: Harry Owiredu-Ashley
机构: 未知
类目: Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: 8 pages, 6 figures. Code and artifacts: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agentic red-teaming benchmarks report whether an injected agent was compromised as a single bit: the attack succeeded, or it did not. We argue that this binary attack-success rate discards the information a defender most needs, namely how harmful the resulting action was. We introduce an action-graded harm rubric that scores an agent’s tool-call trajectory on a seven-level ordinal scale (L0 to L6) according to whether the executed action was reversible, whether it crossed scope to reach another party, and whether it expanded privilege. We compute the scale two ways: a deterministic oracle that reads the trajectory and the attacker’s stated goal, and a panel of three frontier language-model judges that read a tag-free account of the same trajectory. Across four victim models and two defenses on the AgentDojo workspace suite, severity grading exposes three cases the binary metric hides, including a defense that reports a zero attack-success rate while still permitting an externally visible cross-scope leak through an unfiltered tool. The judge panel reproduces the oracle with high ordinal agreement (Krippendorff’s alpha = 0.91) but shares systematic blind spots that we characterize, most notably a failure to recognize escalation chains. Unlike prior work that provides harm taxonomies, harmful-task completion tests, execution-level safety benchmarks, or severity-aware simulation, our contribution is a reusable, trace-grounded severity instrument applied to the actual actions recorded in existing red-team logs. All code, prompts, and per-episode logs are released.

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大规模电商场景下属性值提取（Attribute Value Extraction, AVE）任务中高质量标注数据稀缺的问题。由于产品类型、属性维度及语言种类的组合规模庞大，人工标注所需成本极高，难以满足工业级应用需求。为此，论文提出SynthAVE，一个涵盖12,726个产品、229种产品类型、792个属性以及西班牙语、法语、意大利语和德语共4种语言的大规模人类验证基准数据集。其核心解决方案是设计一种多大语言模型（multi-LLM）竞技场框架（arena framework），通过21种独立的判别配置（7个模型族 × 3种提示模板）对合成标签进行并行评估，并采用多数投票机制确定最终标签。实验表明，该集成方法与人类专家的一致性达到Cohen’s κ = 0.92（95.2%一致率），单个模型间也表现出较高的一致性（Fleiss’ κ = 0.76）。这证明了多样化模型在个体判断差异基础上可通过集成实现高可靠性预测，从而在保证与人工标注相当质量的前提下，实现低成本、可扩展的合成标签验证。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07469
作者: Andrea Scarinci,Virginia Negri,Brayan Impata,Suleiman Khan,Victor Martinez,Marcello Federico

Abstract:Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) for e-commerce attribute extraction requires labeled data representative across thousands of product types, attributes, and multiple languages. This combinatorial scale translates to millions of annotations, rendering human labeling prohibitively costly. While recent work has demonstrated synthetic label generation using LLMs, deploying such approaches at industrial scale requires integrated quality control mechanisms. We present SynthAVE, a large-scale human-validated benchmark for attribute value extraction spanning 12,726 products across 229 product types, 792 attributes, and 4 languages (Spanish, French, Italian, German). To validate synthetic labels at scale, we introduce a multi-LLM arena framework where samples are independently evaluated by 21 judge configurations (7 model families \times 3 prompts), with final labels determined via majority voting. The majority vote ensemble agrees with human experts at Cohen’s \kappa = 0.92 (95.2% agreement), while individual judges show substantial inter-model agreement (Fleiss’ \kappa = 0.76 ). This demonstrates that diverse models with varying individual judgments aggregate into highly reliable predictions, enabling cost-effective validation at scale while maintaining quality parity with human review.

[NLP-14] he Blind Curator: How a Biased Judge Silently Disables Skill Retirement in Self-Evolving Agents

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决在大语言模型（LLM）作为裁判（judge）执行无参考评估任务时，因裁判偏倚导致技能退役机制失效的问题。其核心挑战在于：生成式系统依赖“技能退役”这一结构约束来防止能力退化至无技能基线，而该机制的有效性依赖于无偏奖励信号，但实际中LLM裁判常引入偏差，尤其表现为“假通过偏倚”（false-pass bias，即失败案例被误判为成功），从而悄然关闭了系统的自我修正能力。论文的关键解决方案是通过受控的奖励污染分析与行为实验，在无参考报告生成测试平台中结合代码生成交叉验证，揭示了假通过偏倚会引发技能退役机制的突变式失效——一旦超过临界阈值，无论增加多少数据都无法恢复淘汰劣质技能的能力。进一步分析表明，这种机制失效具有普适性，仅在接近零假通过率、具备验证器特性的裁判中得以避免。尽管系统整体评估质量在某些情况下仍保持稳定，但其根本安全机制已“静默失效”，无法通过聚合指标暴露。因此，该研究贡献在于提出一种行为安全性结论而非性能提升，并设计了一种低成本的缺陷注入审计方法，可在部署前识别裁判是否处于临界阈值的危险侧。

Abstract:A self-evolving agent retires its bad skills by watching them fail, so what happens when the judge cannot see the failures? Skill retirement is the structural constraint that keeps a growing library from drifting below the no-skill baseline, but its guarantee assumes an unbiased reward, which is false for the LLM judges that reference-free tasks force upon us. We show that a biased judge does not merely add noise; it \emphsilently switches off the curator. We make this precise with a corrupted-reward analysis and, isolating the causal channel by injecting corruption on top of a deterministic reward, a behavioral study on a reference-free report-writing testbed with a code-generation cross-check. Symmetric noise leaves retirement intact, but \emphfalse-pass bias (failures slipping through as passes) disables contribution-based retirement past a sharp threshold that no amount of data can cross. Separating genuine retirement from cap-eviction churn shows this \emphmechanism failure is universal, holding across domains and failure rates and sparing only near-zero-false-pass, verifier-like graders. The downstream \emphoutcome, though, is regime-dependent: eval quality degrades only where the same corruption also starves skill synthesis, and otherwise holds steady, so the disabled curator is \emphsilent, surfacing in no aggregate metric. The contribution is a behavioral safety result, not a performance one. A cheap defect-injection audit then tells an operator, before deployment, which side of the threshold their judge occupies.

[NLP-15] DeLS-Spec: Decoupled Long-Short Contexts for Parallel Speculative Drafting

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决块并行推测解码（block-parallel speculative decoding）中长期上下文建模与局部因果依赖性之间的矛盾问题。现有方法如DFlash虽通过单次前向传播预测整个块以提升效率，但其位置无关的预测缺乏显式的块内因果约束，导致生成质量下降；而后续方法如Domino和DSpark虽引入了因果性，却需从头训练推测模型，带来高昂的训练成本且灵活性不足。本文提出DeLS-Spec，一种解耦的长短期上下文推测解码方法，其核心创新在于将固定预训练的DFlash模型作为长上下文专家，同时引入一个轻量级的局部头（local head）作为短上下文专家。该局部头可独立使用标准的下一个词预测目标进行训练，无需与目标模型或DFlash主干联合训练，从而实现极低的训练开销。在推理阶段，DeLS-Spec融合长、短上下文的输出概率分布，且局部头不绑定特定的DFlash检查点，显著提升了系统的模块化与灵活性。实验结果表明，在Qwen3模型上，DeLS-Spec在数学、代码和对话等多类基准任务中均持续优于DFlash，实现了更高的加速比与平均接受长度。

Abstract:Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting multiple tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-parallel drafters such as DFlash further improve drafting efficiency by predicting an entire block in one pass, but their position-wise predictions lack explicit intra-block causal conditioning. Recent methods such as Domino and DSpark attempt to introduce such causality into block-parallel drafting, but they require training the draft model from scratch, which limits their flexibility and increases training cost. We propose DeLS-Spec, a decoupled long-short context speculative decoding method. DeLS-Spec treats the fixed DFlash model as a long-context expert and introduces a lightweight local head as a short-context expert. The local head can be trained independently with a standard next-token prediction objective, without joint training with the target model or the DFlash backbone, leading to extremely low training cost. At inference time, DeLS-Spec combines long-context and short-context logits, and the local head is not tied to a specific DFlash checkpoint, making the method more modular and flexible. Experiments on Qwen3 models show that DeLS-Spec consistently improves speedup and average acceptance length over DFlash across math, code, and dialogue benchmarks.

Abstract:Automatic prosodic segmentation identifies boundaries between speech units from acoustic and linguistic evidence. [NLP-17] F-Engram: A Train-Free Engram with SSD-Backed Memory for Large Language Models

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大语言模型（Large Language Models, LLMs）在知识扩展过程中因依赖预训练、微调、检索增强或更长上下文而带来的高成本问题。现有基于记忆（Engram-style memory）的方案虽能以紧凑方式注入隐藏状态，但依赖哈希压缩的GPU驻留设计常导致不相关短语在共享槽位中发生冲突，损害短语级语义保真度。为此，论文提出TF-Engram，一种无需训练的Engram系统：其关键在于离线从外部语料构建针对特定短语的语义记忆表，将大规模记忆表跨部署于GPU–DRAM–SSD分层存储架构中，并采用早期退出引导的预测性预取（Early-Exit Guided Predictive Prefetching）机制，在自回归解码过程中隐藏外部内存访问延迟。实验表明，该方法在Qwen3-0.6B上将下游平均得分从57.6提升至59.4，优于冻结主干网络及参数量相当的LoRA基线；系统评估进一步验证了大规模记忆表可实现较低开销的离线构建、基于SSD的存储显著降低GPU内存占用，且预测预取有效缓解了外部内存访问带来的吞吐损失。结果表明，静态短语记忆可作为可扩展、免训练、低开销的组件集成至LLM推理流程中。

作者: Yutang Ma,Kecheng Huang,Xikun Jiang,Zili Shao
机构: The Chinese University of Hong Kong (香港中文大学); Beijing Institute of Technology, Zhuhai (北京理工大学珠海学院)

Abstract:Large Language Models (LLMs) store factual knowledge and domain-specific patterns implicitly in dense Transformer parameters, making knowledge expansion costly through pretraining, fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, or longer contexts. Engram-style memory offers a compact hidden-state injection pathway, but existing GPU-resident designs often rely on hash-based compression, causing unrelated phrases to collide in shared slots and weakening phrase-level semantic fidelity. We present TF-Engram, a train-free Engram system that constructs phrase-specific semantic memory offline from external corpora, stores large memory tables across a GPU–DRAM–SSD hierarchy, and uses Early-Exit Guided Predictive Prefetching to hide external-memory latency during autoregressive decoding. System evaluation shows that large TF-Engram tables can be built with moderate offline cost, SSD-backed storage substantially reduces GPU memory demand, and predictive prefetching recovers much of the throughput loss caused by external memory access. These results demonstrate that static phrase memory can be integrated into LLM inference as a scalable, train-free, and low-overhead system component.

[NLP-20] Multimodal Voice Activity Projection for Turn-Taking in Social Robots with Voice-Activity-Related Pretrained Encoders DATE

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决社交机器人在人类-人类互动场景中（尤其是调解型交互）的对话轮换预测问题，核心挑战在于机器人需主动预判对话动态而非仅响应沉默。其解决方案的关键在于提出一种多模态语音活动投影（Multimodal Voice Activity Projection, MM-VAP）框架，该框架将原始仅基于音频的语音活动投影（VAP）模型扩展至同步的音视频输入，同时保持自监督的未来投影目标。该方法利用预先训练的音视频骨干网络（针对语音任务优化），通过低秩适应（Low-Rank Adaptation, LoRA）进行微调，并在独立说话人编码后引入跨说话人注意力机制，以建模对话双方之间的关系动态，从而实现对未来语音活动的准确预测。此外，引入语义一致性损失（semantic consistency loss），对256维输出空间进行正则化，使其与高层对话行为模式保持一致。实验结果表明，该方法在NoXi和NoXi+J数据集上优于现有基线，尤其在部分轮换事件上表现显著提升；在Haru EDR语料库上的额外评估进一步验证了其在面向调解任务的人机交互中的适用性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07294
作者: Antonio Cano,Guillermo Pérez,Luis Merino,Randy Gomez
机构: 4i Intelligent Insights(4i智能洞察); Universidad de Sevilla(塞维利亚大学); Universidad Pablo de Olavide(帕博·德·奥拉维德大学); Honda Research Institute Japan(本田研究院日本)
类目: Robotics (cs.RO); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: Accepted for presentation at the 35th IEEE International Conference on Robot and Human Interactive Communication (RO-MAN 2026). Acceptance notification date: 30 May 2026. Final published version pending

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Turn-taking prediction is a key requirement for social robots involved in human-human interaction, particularly in mediator settings, where the robot must anticipate conversational dynamics rather than merely react to pauses. This work presents a Multimodal Voice Activity Projection (MM-VAP) framework that extends the original audio-only VAP formulation to synchronized audio-visual inputs while preserving its self-supervised future-projection objective. The proposed approach builds on pretrained audio-visual backbones originally optimized for speech-related tasks and adapts them through Low-Rank Adaptation to the multimodal turn-taking problem. After independent speaker encoding, an inter-speaker attention stage models the relational dynamics required to project future voice activity. In addition, a semantic consistency loss is introduced to regularize the 256-state output space according to higher-level dialogue activity patterns. Experiments on NoXi and NoXi+J showed improvements over the current baselines, particularly for some turn-taking events. Additional evaluation on the Haru EDR corpus further supported the suitability of this direction for mediation-oriented human-robot interaction.

[NLP-21] A Word-Level Digital Reader of the Prasthanatrayi with Sankaras Bhasya: Corpus Method and an Open Offline Reading Aid for the Advaita Vedanta Canon

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决印度教不二论（Advaita Vedanta）核心典籍——“普拉斯坦纳特拉伊”（Prasthanatrayi，即十部主要《奥义书》、《梵经》及《薄伽梵歌》及其商羯罗（Sankara）注释）在文本阅读中因连续音变（sandhi）、复杂复合词（samasa）和密集的学术性散文表达而导致的词级解析困难问题。其核心挑战在于：文本中词与词之间的边界模糊，词形变化与音变规则掩盖了词根与语法功能，严重阻碍了精确的语义理解与研究。本文提出的关键解决方案是构建一个完全离线、开源的词级数字阅读器，支持对整个普拉斯坦纳特拉伊及其商羯罗注疏进行逐词点击解析。该系统通过一个混合式处理流程实现高精度分析：基于规则的音变拆分器结合词形词典与实证语料库查表，并引入大语言模型（LLM）辅助分析，在对抗性双重验证协议下确保可靠性；同时建立持久的人工审核闭环，确保校正结果在每次重新生成时均被保留。该工具将所有词汇映射至词元（lemma），不仅实现词形切分（padaccheda）、形态学分析与释义弹窗，还具备跨层次（原文与注疏）的词频检索功能，形成一个可检索的词频对照表（concordance）。评估结果显示，99%以上的实证词形分析与权威词形词典高度一致，且错误集中于低置信度层级，符合预期分布，验证了系统的可靠性。最终产品为一个独立的单文件HTML应用，无需服务器或网络支持，可自由分发，作为教学与研读工具。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07282
作者: Tamal Maharaj
机构: Ramakrishna Mission Vivekananda Educational and Research Institute (梵音教育与研究学院)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Digital Libraries (cs.DL)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The Prasthanatrayi – the ten principal Upanisads, the Brahmasutra, and the Bhagavadgita, with Sankara’s commentaries (bhasya) – is the foundational corpus of Advaita Vedanta. Continuous euphonic combination (sandhi), long compounds (samasa), and dense scholastic prose make it hard to read at the word level: where one word ends, and what each word means grammatically, are both obscured. We present an open, fully offline, word-level digital reader of the entire Prasthanatrayi with Sankara’s bhasya. Every word – of both the root text (mula) and the commentary – is clickable and resolves to a pop-up giving its split (padaccheda), morphological analysis, and gloss. Because every word carries a lemma, the reader also acts as a concordance: a search on a dictionary headword retrieves all of that word’s inflected and sandhi-hidden occurrences, and its occurrences inside compounds, across both layers. The resource covers thirteen commentarial units (2,971 verses, sutras, and prose sections; 36,881 analysed word-occurrences of root text) and a global dictionary of 95,587 distinct commentarial surface forms. We describe the corpus, the hybrid pipeline – a rule-based sandhi splitter over an inflected-form lexicon and attested-corpus look-ups, with LLM-assisted analysis under an adversarial two-pass verification protocol – and a durable human-review loop whose corrections survive every regeneration. An intrinsic evaluation against independent Sanskrit resources finds high-confidence analyses agree with an authoritative inflectional lexicon on over 99% of attested forms, and a band-blind adjudication confirms that quality degrades predictably across confidence bands, with errors concentrated in the low-confidence tier the review loop targets. The reader is a single self-contained HTML file needing no server or network, offered as a freely redistributable teaching and reading aid.

[NLP-22] Understanding Interpretation Difficulty in Harmful Online Communication: Insights from Cybercrime Communities

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决网络有害内容中因使用俚语、隐晦表达、缩写及社群特有术语而导致的语义解读困难问题，尤其聚焦于与网络犯罪相关的Discord聊天内容。其核心解决方案在于将解读过程视为一个证据整合（evidence-integration）问题，而非仅依赖消息级别的分类。研究通过构建专家审定的参考解读，评估人类与大语言模型（Large Language Model, LLM）在不同上下文条件下的解读表现，发现局部上下文对人类解读能力提升有限，而外部知识和扩展对话上下文能显著改善理解；对于LLM而言，局部上下文同样有助于提升解读效果，且模型规模越大性能越优。进一步的定性错误分析提出了导致有害聊天难以解读的若干因素分类，强调在有害内容分析中应综合多源信息以实现更准确的理解。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07277
作者: Tomohiro Okatsu,Naoki Takada,Yin Min Pa Pa,Katsunari Yoshioka,Tatsunori Mori
机构: Yokohama National University, Japan
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computers and Society (cs.CY)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Harmful online communication often contains slang, coded terms, abbreviations, and community-specific expressions, which make messages difficult to interpret. This paper presents an exploratory study of interpretation difficulty in Discord chats related to cybercrime. We construct reference interpretations of purposefully selected difficult messages, which were reviewed by an expert. We then use them to evaluate human and large language model (LLM) interpretations under different context conditions. The results show that local context alone is often insufficient for humans, while external knowledge and extended conversational context substantially improve human interpretation. For LLMs, local context also improves interpretation, and the larger model performs better. We further conduct a qualitative error analysis and propose a preliminary classification of factors that make harmful chats difficult to interpret. These findings suggest that harmful-content analysis should treat interpretation as an evidence-integration problem, rather than as message-level classification alone.

[NLP-23] Billions of Sketches Reveal Hidden Cultural Variation in Human Concepts

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决人类概念普遍性（conceptual universality）研究中长期依赖语言相似性评估所导致的潜在偏差问题。传统方法通过跨语言词汇比较来推断概念的普遍性，但语言作为交流工具会将丰富的个体与文化体验压缩为共享惯例，从而掩盖了概念在心智表征中的深层差异。为此，论文提出以大规模视觉数据为切入点，分析来自236个主权国家和地区、总计26亿张由人类绘制的常见概念草图，从视觉想象角度揭示概念结构的内在多样性。其核心解决方案在于利用草图嵌入模型（sketch embedding models）与词嵌入模型（word embedding models）的对比，发现视觉表征能够保留更丰富的语义与文化结构信息，而语言模型则倾向于压缩这些细节。研究显示，基于草图的跨文化相似性与已知文化距离的相关性比文本测量高出45%，且涉及触觉交互的概念表现出更强的文化差异，表明视觉意象不仅反映符号约定，更映射了具身经验（embodied experience）的多样性。因此，该研究的关键突破在于证明：概念普遍性的模式高度依赖于测量模态，而大规模草图数据提供了一种直接、高分辨率的工具，可有效探测思想在具身化与文化维度上的深层多样性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07267
作者: Arianna Pera,Mauro Martino,Nima Dehmamy,Douglas Guilbeault,Luca Maria Aiello,Andrea Baronchelli
机构: 未知
类目: Computers and Society (cs.CY); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Physics and Society (physics.soc-ph)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Claims about the universality of human concepts have been predominantly assessed through linguistic similarity across languages and cultures. However, words are effective as communication devices because they compress rich experiential variation into shared conventions, potentially obscuring hidden individual and cultural differences in how concepts are mentally represented. Here, we analyse 2.6 billion human-made sketches of common concepts from 236 countries and territories to examine conceptual structure through people’s visual imagination. Consistent with recent work on image-based cognition, we find that single concepts unfold into multiple distinct visual exemplars, revealing latent information about similarities and differences in conceptual structure across cultures. This variation is strongest for concepts involving haptic interaction, suggesting that visual imagery reflects variation in embodied experience as much as conventional definitions. Comparing embedding models of sketches with word embedding models across languages, we find that their geometries diverge, with visual representations preserving rich semantic and cultural structure that language models compress. Cross-cultural similarities derived from sketches align 45% more closely with established cultural distances than do text-based measures. Together, these results suggest that patterns of human conceptual universality may depend critically on the modality through which concepts are measured, with large-scale sketching providing a direct, high-resolution probe of conceptual diversity across embodied and cultural dimensions of thought.

[NLP-24] Evaluation of Multilingual Ability to Use Spatial Deictic Expressions in Vision-Language Models ACL

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决视觉语言模型（VLMs）在多语言环境下对空间指示性表达（spatial deictic expressions）的准确理解和使用能力不足的问题。空间指示性表达（如“this”和“that”）的指代对象依赖于具体语境，要求模型能够联合推理语言信息与视觉空间结构，实现对上下文相关指代的精准定位。此外，不同语言中空间指示词的语义区分具有语言特异性，模型需具备跨语言理解此类空间差异的能力。为此，论文构建了一个涵盖四种语言的基准测试集，用于评估VLMs在多语言场景下对空间指示性表达的运用能力。实验结果表明，现有模型在基于物体距离选择恰当指示词方面表现显著偏离人类认知模式，其核心问题在于缺乏对视觉空间结构与语言语用规则之间深层关联的建模能力。解决方案的关键在于提升模型对视觉空间上下文的精细感知能力，并强化其对多语言中空间指代差异的语义理解能力。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07251
作者: Kaito Watanabe,Taisei Yamamoto,Tomoki Doi,Hitomi Yanaka
机构: The University of Tokyo(东京大学); Riken(理化学研究所); Tohoku University(东北大学)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: Accepted to ACL SRW 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:One of the expected abilities of vision-language models (VLMs) is spatial reasoning ability based on a given text and image. To evaluate the spatial reasoning abilities of VLMs, we focus on the use of spatial deictic expressions, which are defined as spatial expressions whose referent is determined by their situational context, such as this'' and that’'. To handle spatial deictic expressions, VLMs must jointly reason over language and visual space, grounding context-dependent references in the image’s spatial structure. In addition, selecting appropriate spatial deictic expressions across languages requires VLMs to understand the language-specific spatial distinctions encoded by these expressions. In this paper, we develop a benchmark to evaluate the multilingual ability of VLMs to use spatial deictic expressions in four languages. Our experiments using this benchmark reveal that the tested models use demonstratives in a manner different from that of humans, particularly in selecting the appropriate demonstratives based on the distance to the object.

[NLP-25] From Text to Parameters: Predicting Item Parameters from Embedding Regularization with Reliability and Design Ceilings

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决项目校准中的“冷启动”问题，即新开发题目的心理测量属性在未经过实地测试前无法确定，导致难以进行有效校准。其解决方案的关键在于利用现代文本嵌入（text embeddings）技术自动构建传统上需人工指定的设计矩阵，并结合正则化回归模型预测题目参数。研究提出了一套综合评估框架，包括基于重复交叉验证的决定系数（R²）及其重抽样标准差、以及两个性能上限：由参数标准误推导出的信度上限（reliability ceiling）和通过模拟功效校准得到的设计上限（design ceiling）。实证分析表明，题目难度可高度由文本特征预测（重复交叉验证R² = 0.53，约为信度上限的57%），而区分度与伪猜测参数的可预测性较低；但通过对比性能上限发现，这种差异主要源于目标参数的可靠性水平而非文本信号强度——文本对所有难度目标均能解释约57%至63%的可靠方差，而三参数逻辑斯蒂模型（3PL）中的伪猜测参数因信度上限接近零，实际上不可靠。此外，在医学执照考试基准（BEA 2024）中，基于嵌入的回归虽解释方差极少，却达到领先榜单的均方根误差（RMSE），凸显了在基准评测中使用无量纲指标与明确性能上限的重要性。最后，研究揭示单次训练-测试划分可能使R²值虚高0.1至0.15，强调了在项目校准支持及未来基准建设中采用重复交叉验证的必要性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07141
作者: Shi-Ting Chen,Jinsong Chen
机构: 未知
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Methodology (stat.ME)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Newly developed items must ordinarily be field tested before their psychometric properties are known, creating a cold start problem for item calibration. Predicting item parameters from features is a long standing measurement problem dating back to the Linear Logistic Test Model; modern text embeddings now automate the design matrices traditionally specified by hand. We propose an evaluation framework combining regularized regression on item text embeddings, repeated cross validated R squared reported with its resampling standard deviation, and two performance upper bounds: a reliability ceiling derived from parameter standard errors, and a design ceiling derived from simulation based power calibration. Applying this framework to a mathematics item bank (EEDI) and a medical licensure benchmark (BEA 2024), we find that item difficulty is highly predictable from text (repeated cross validated R squared = 0.53, or about 57% of its reliability ceiling), whereas discrimination and pseudo guessing appear less predictable. However, evaluating these results against our ceilings reveals that this apparent hierarchy stems from target reliability rather than text signal strength: text uniformly recovers 57 to 63% of the reliable variance across difficulty targets, whereas the 3PL pseudo guessing parameter has a reliability ceiling near zero, making it an unviable target at current precision. On BEA, embedding based regression matches leaderboard RMSE despite explaining almost no variance, highlighting the critical need for scale free metrics and explicit ceilings in benchmarking. Finally, we show that a single train and test split can inflate apparent accuracy by 0.1 to 0.15 in R squared, underscoring the necessity of repeated cross validation for calibration support applications and future benchmark construction.

[NLP-26] Behavior Leverag e Imbalance in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决多教师在线策略蒸馏（multi-teacher on-policy distillation, OPD）在生成式语言模型工具调用任务中出现的“行为偏差”问题，即模型在训练过程中因教师信号分布不均衡而产生过度调用工具（over-calling）的现象。尽管整体损失函数表现良好，但模型在具体决策行为上出现了不可见的退化，表现为对本应直接回答的任务仍频繁调用工具。其解决方案的关键在于识别并缓解“行为杠杆失衡”（behavior leverage imbalance）——即在生成过程中，某些特定位置的局部token级信号（如tool_call、函数名等）对全局生成模式具有过强控制力，导致模型偏离正确行为。为此，作者提出一种名为Soft Clamp的逐token发散校准方法，通过动态压缩极端的Jensen-Shannon发散值，同时保留非零梯度，从而在不牺牲决策准确率的前提下，有效降低过调用率（从13.7%降至9.0%），并减少工具调用循环和重复调用。研究强调，多教师OPD应关注教师信号在生成过程中的作用位置与机制，而不仅依赖于聚合层面的损失指标。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07050
作者: Jiabin Shen,Guang Chen,Chengjun Mao
机构: Ant Group (蚂蚁集团)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注: 17 pages including appendix, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agentic language models must learn when to call tools, when to consume tool responses, and when to answer directly. This makes multi-teacher on-policy distillation a natural training strategy: one teacher can specialize in tool calls, another in direct responses, and the student can learn from both on its own generated distribution. We show that this strategy can induce a behavior shift that is invisible from aggregate losses alone. In a two-teacher tool-use setting, vanilla generalized knowledge distillation improves tool-call recall but also moves the model toward over-calling, where it calls tools on examples that should be answered directly. Aggregate explanations are insufficient: tool-call samples do not receive more token exposure, and full-sequence per-token divergence is not larger for the tool-call teacher. We instead analyze behavior leverage imbalance: local token-level signals at mode- entry and structural positions, such as tool_call and function names, can have disproportionate control over the global generation mode. We propose Soft Clamp, a per-token divergence calibration method that dynamically compresses extreme token-level Jensen-Shannon divergence while preserving nonzero gradients. On APIGen-MT, Soft Clamp reduces over-calling from 13.7% to 9.0% relative to vanilla GKD while matching its decision accuracy. In a BFCL multi-turn diagnostic, it also lowers tool-call loops and repeated calls among GKD variants. These results suggest that multi-teacher OPD should monitor where teacher signals act, not only how large they are in aggregate.
